# The Nomad

Journal: `quests/main_quest/prologue/q000_nomad` · `MainQuest` · hash `388514850`.

Journal premise: A good kid from Heywood with stolen corporate cargo in hand, border guards breathing down your neck and a brutal heat haze melting the horizon. This is what freedom looks like. And if smuggling's what it takes to live it, then smuggling it is. But if that's not motivation enough, there's a fat stack of eddies waiting at the end of it too. All that needs doin' is moving the package from A to B. Simple.

**Coverage: journal-linked.** 123 exact journal-linked nodes in 15 resources; 18 resources in the inspected dependency context.

Resource roles in that context: 16 content candidate, 2 repair. Role labels describe path/name evidence, not proof of runtime reachability.

Root quest resources with additional journal references: none indexed. Roots remain activation context when a phase/scene anchor exists, to avoid importing sibling launch scaffolding.

Generated structural analysis from decoded resources. Shared resources are context, not exclusive quest ownership. The tables trace stored sockets and fact dependencies; they do not simulate runtime ordering, prove all branches reachable, or infer chronology from journal objective order. See [method](../all-quests-method.md).

| Yo, V, every story's gotta start somewhere, right? Even if it means that "somewhere" is in the middle of nowhere and the most nova place to hang is a dusty-ass fuel stop. You abandoned your nomad family to come find the dude who hired you, to smuggle something for him 'cross the border. This guy will change your life, mano. World NodeRef definitions/streaming owners, full actor and animation closure, numeric TweakDB names, spoken subtitle/VO payload joins and installed/save behavior have not been validated by this structural pass. Raw direct references, actor bindings, choices, event locstrings and community entries remain in the evidence shards.

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.

## Connected paths and supporting bindings

967 repeated typed/socket path signatures in this selected graph context; 181 distinct direct resource identities; 23 embedded graph handoffs. The full occurrence lists, concrete parameters, exact source pointers and external edges are retained in the checked evidence. Shared forward dependencies can serve several quests; these are context counts.
